Its executable is `ledger` at the\napplication root. Every command reaches the database only through the generated persistence package,\nruns as one transaction, and writes CSV to standard output with diagnostics on standard error.\n\n## Layout\n\n| Path | Owner | Purpose |\n|------|-------|---------|\n| `ledger` | cli | Executable at the application root that dispatches each command. |\n| `cli/` | cli | Command dispatch, CSV output, exit codes, and usage errors. |\n| `amounts.py` | amounts | Conversion between integer cents and two-decimal amount text. |\n| `config.py` | config | Resolution of `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| `persistence/` | generated | Shipped persistence library plus the declaration generated from `DATABASE.md`. |\n| `schema.sql` | generated | Idempotent DDL generated from `DATABASE.md`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | test runner | POSIX runner for the project suite with a slice selector. |\n| `tests/` | project suite | pytest tests written beside the code. |\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`, `.venv/` | language foundation | Dependency manifest, lockfile, and environment. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level access permitted |\n|----------|-------|----------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` package | `sqlite3` and `psycopg` are reached only inside `persistence/`. |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | Reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH` from the environment. |\n| File store | None | The product has no file store beyond the database. |\n| External service | None | Network access only to the PostgreSQL server named by `DATABASE_URL`. |\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer: implementation language and pytest test runner (`python.md`).\n- SQLite: default database engine, reached through the persistence library (`sqlite.md`).\n- PostgreSQL: engine selected when `DATABASE_URL` is a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN,\n  with `psycopg[binary]` as the driver (`postgres.md`). Variance: `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md` does not list\n  PostgreSQL, but `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` requires it. The sources govern, per decision Q-001.\n- CLI: the `ledger` executable (`cli.md`).\n- Shell: POSIX `sh` for `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader (`common.md`).\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Amounts are stored as integer cents. No layer uses floating point for money.\n- Every command runs as one database transaction through `open_database(dsn)`.\n- `pyproject.toml` declares Python 3.11 or newer, `pytest`, and `psycopg[binary]`. `uv.lock` is\n  committed. `.venv` is created by `uv venv`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC scaffold-pyproject ===\nIntent: The project manifest declares Python 3.11 or newer, pytest, and psycopg.\n\nimport os\nimport re\nimport tomllib\n\nwith open(\"pyproject.toml\", \"rb\") as handle:\n    manifest = tomllib.load(handle)\nproject = manifest[\"project\"]\nrequirement = project[\"requires-python\"]\ndependencies = project[\"dependencies\"]\nnames = {re.split(r\"[<>=!~\\[ ;]\", entry, maxsplit=1)[0].lower() for entry in dependencies}\nprint(requirement, sorted(names))\nassert requirement.startswith(\">=3.11\")\nassert {\"pytest\", \"psycopg\"} <= names\n=== END AC scaffold-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC scaffold-environment ===\nIntent: The lockfile is committed and the virtual environment directory exists.\n\nimport os\n\nassert os.path.isfile(\"uv.lock\")\nassert os.path.isdir(\".venv\")\nassert os.path.isfile(\"pyproject.toml\")\n=== END AC scaffold-environment ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `persistence/`, `schema.sql`, and the persistence declaration are generated by Drydock and are\n  never hand-written.\n- No module outside `persistence/` imports `sqlite3` or `psycopg`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TEST-RUNNER.md ===\n# FEATURE: Test Runner\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | `bin/test.sh` runs the project suite against a temporary database and accepts an optional slice selector. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | `sh bin/test.sh [SLICE]` |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`bin/test.sh` is the single entry point for the project's automated tests. Story-level builds run\na slice of the suite. The terminal story runs the whole suite.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `sh bin/test.sh` runs `pytest -q` from the application root with no selector.\n- `sh bin/test.sh SLICE` passes `SLICE` to pytest as a `-k` expression, narrowing the run to the\n  matching tests.\n- Before running pytest, the script exports `DATABASE_URL` pointing at a fresh temporary SQLite\n  file, so tests never touch the user's database.\n- The script exits `0` only when every selected test passes, and a nonzero status otherwise.\n- The script uses the interpreter in `.venv` when it exists.\n- The script is POSIX `sh`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC test-runner-syntax ===\nIntent: The test runner is valid POSIX shell syntax.\n\nimport subprocess\n\nresult = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"-n\", \"bin/test.sh\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC test-runner-syntax ===\n\n=== AC test-runner-selector ===\nIntent: The slice selector runs only the matching tests and reports their result in the exit status.\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\n\nprobes = {\n    \"tests/test_ac_probe_pass.py\": \"def test_probe_pass():\\n    assert True\\n\",\n    \"tests/test_ac_probe_fail.py\": \"def test_probe_fail():\\n    assert False\\n\",\n}\nos.makedirs(\"tests\", exist_ok=True)\ntry:\n    for path, source in probes.items():\n        with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n            handle.write(source)\n    passing = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"bin/test.sh\", \"probe_pass\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    failing = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"bin/test.sh\", \"probe_fail\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nfinally:\n    for path in probes:\n        if os.path.exists(path):\n            os.remove(path)\nprint(passing.stdout)\nprint(passing.stderr)\nprint(failing.stdout)\nprint(failing.stderr)\nassert passing.returncode == 0\nassert failing.returncode != 0\n=== END AC test-runner-selector ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `bin/test.sh` uses POSIX `sh` only. Resolution lives in\n`config.py`, and the persistence library then selects the engine from the DSN.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `resolve_dsn(environ)` returns the value of `DATABASE_URL` when it is set and non-empty.\n- Otherwise it returns the value of `DATABASE_PATH` when that is set and non-empty.\n- Otherwise it returns `data/app.db`.\n- The returned value is the configured string unchanged. No normalization is applied.\n- A `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN selects PostgreSQL. Any other value selects SQLite, with an\n  optional `sqlite://` prefix. The persistence library makes that choice.\n- Connection failures and expired connect attempts surface as `PersistenceError`, so the command\n  layer can exit `1`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC db-selection-url-precedence ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL wins over DATABASE_PATH when both are set, and the value is returned unchanged.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nurl = os.path.join(directory, \"url.db\")\npath = os.path.join(directory, \"path.db\")\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": url, \"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == url\n=== END AC db-selection-url-precedence ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-path-fallback ===\nIntent: DATABASE_PATH is used when DATABASE_URL is unset or empty.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(directory, \"path.db\")\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == path\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": \"\", \"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == path\n=== END AC db-selection-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-postgres-dsn ===\nIntent: A PostgreSQL DSN in DATABASE_URL is returned unchanged for the persistence library to select the engine.\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndsn = \"postgresql://127.0.0.1:1/ledger\"\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": dsn}) == dsn\n=== END AC db-selection-postgres-dsn ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-default ===\nIntent: With neither variable set, resolution returns a non-empty database location.\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\nresult = resolve_dsn({})\nassert isinstance(result, str)\nassert result != \"\"\n=== END AC db-selection-default ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-unreachable ===\nIntent: An unreachable PostgreSQL server raises PersistenceError instead of returning a database.\n\nRequires: python-package=psycopg; scope=runtime\n\nfrom persistence import PersistenceError, open_database\n\ndsn = \"postgresql://127.0.0.1:1/ledger\"\ntry:\n    db = open_database(dsn)\n    db.accounts.list()\nexcept PersistenceError as error:\n    failure = error\nelse:\n    failure = None\nprint(failure)\nassert isinstance(failure, PersistenceError)\n=== END AC db-selection-unreachable ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `config.py` is the only module that reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH`.\n- Diagnostics never print a DSN password to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-AMOUNT-CENTS.md ===\n# FEATURE: Amount Cents\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Converts between validated amount text and integer cents using integer arithmetic only. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TEST-RUNNER.md |\n| Provides    | parse_amount(text) -> int; format_cents(cents) -> str |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAmounts are stored as integer cents and shown with two decimal places. This module is the only place\nthat converts between the two forms.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `parse_amount(text)` accepts text matching `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, with at most two decimal places, and\n  returns the signed integer cent value. `12.5` is `1250` cents.\n- `parse_amount` raises `ValueError` for any other text, including text with more than two decimal\n  places and text containing a comma.\n- `format_cents(cents)` returns text with exactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative,\n  and no thousands separator.\n- All arithmetic is integer arithmetic. No float or `Decimal` conversion is used at any point.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC amount-round-trip ===\nIntent: Formatting then parsing returns the original integer cents, and parse returns an int.\n\nfrom amounts import format_cents, parse_amount\n\nfor cents in (0, 1, -1, 1250, -1250, 500000, -2299):\n    text = format_cents(cents)\n    assert isinstance(text, str)\n    assert parse_amount(text) == cents\n    assert type(parse_amount(text)) is int\n=== END AC amount-round-trip ===\n\n=== AC amount-format-shape ===\nIntent: format_cents writes exactly two decimal places, a leading minus only when negative, and no thousands separator.\n\nfrom amounts import format_cents\n\nfor cents in (0, 5, -5, 1250, 500000, -1250000):\n    text = format_cents(cents)\n    whole, dot, fraction = text.lstrip(\"-\").partition(\".\")\n    assert dot == \".\"\n    assert len(fraction) == 2\n    assert fraction.isdigit()\n    assert whole.isdigit()\n    assert \",\" not in text\n    assert text.startswith(\"-\") == (cents < 0)\n=== END AC amount-format-shape ===\n\n=== AC amount-parse-decimal ===\nIntent: parse_amount reads a signed one-decimal amount as integer cents.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"-12.5\"\nassert parse_amount(text) == -1250\n=== END AC amount-parse-decimal ===\n\n=== AC amount-rejects-three-decimals ===\nIntent: parse_amount rejects text with more than two decimal places by raising ValueError.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"1.234\"\ntry:\n    parse_amount(text)\nexcept ValueError:\n    rejected = True\nelse:\n    rejected = False\nassert rejected is True\n=== END AC amount-rejects-three-decimals ===\n\n=== AC amount-rejects-separator ===\nIntent: parse_amount rejects text containing a thousands separator by raising ValueError.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"1,000.00\"\ntry:\n    parse_amount(text)\nexcept ValueError:\n    rejected = True\nelse:\n    rejected = False\nassert rejected is True\n=== END AC amount-rejects-separator ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never convert money through `float` at any layer.\n- Output amounts never carry a thousands separator.\n=== END ARTIFACT program. Its executable is `ledger` at the\napplication root. Every command reaches the database only through the generated persistence package,\nruns as one transaction, and writes CSV to standard output with diagnostics on standard error.\n\n## Layout\n\n| Path | Owner | Purpose |\n|------|-------|---------|\n| `ledger` | cli | Executable at the application root that dispatches each command. |\n| `cli/` | cli | Command dispatch, CSV output, exit codes, and usage errors. |\n| `amounts.py` | amounts | Conversion between integer cents and two-decimal amount text. |\n| `config.py` | config | Resolution of `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| `persistence/` | generated | Shipped persistence library plus the declaration generated from `DATABASE.md`. |\n| `schema.sql` | generated | Idempotent DDL generated from `DATABASE.md`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | test runner | POSIX runner for the project suite with a slice selector. |\n| `tests/` | project suite | pytest tests written beside the code. |\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`, `.venv/` | language foundation | Dependency manifest, lockfile, and environment. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level access permitted |\n|----------|-------|----------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` package | `sqlite3` and `psycopg` are reached only inside `persistence/`. |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | Reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH` from the environment. |\n| File store | None | The product has no file store beyond the database. |\n| External service | None | Network access only to the PostgreSQL server named by `DATABASE_URL`. |\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer: implementation language and pytest test runner (`python.md`).\n- SQLite: default database engine, reached through the persistence library (`sqlite.md`).\n- PostgreSQL: engine selected when `DATABASE_URL` is a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN,\n  with `psycopg[binary]` as the driver (`postgres.md`). Variance: `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md` does not list\n  PostgreSQL, but `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` requires it. No layer uses floating point for money.\n- Every command runs as one database transaction through `open_database(dsn)`.\n- `pyproject.toml` declares Python 3.11 or newer, `pytest`, and `psycopg[binary]`. `uv.lock` is\n  committed. `.venv` is created by `uv venv`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC scaffold-pyproject ===\nIntent: The project manifest declares Python 3.11 or newer, pytest, and psycopg.\n\nimport os\nimport re\nimport tomllib\n\nwith open(\"pyproject.toml\", \"rb\") as handle:\n    manifest = tomllib.load(handle)\nproject = manifest[\"project\"]\nrequirement = project[\"requires-python\"]\ndependencies = project[\"dependencies\"]\nnames = {re.split(r\"[<>=!~\\[ ;]\", entry, maxsplit=1)[0].lower() for entry in dependencies}\nprint(requirement, sorted(names))\nassert requirement.startswith(\">=3.11\")\nassert {\"pytest\", \"psycopg\"} <= names\n=== END AC scaffold-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC scaffold-environment ===\nIntent: The lockfile is committed and the virtual environment directory exists.\n\nimport os\n\nassert os.path.isfile(\"uv.lock\")\nassert os.path.isdir(\".venv\")\nassert os.path.isfile(\"pyproject.toml\")\n=== END AC scaffold-environment ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `persistence/`, `schema.sql`, and the persistence declaration are generated by Drydock and are\n  never hand-written.\n- No module outside `persistence/` imports `sqlite3` or `psycopg`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TEST-RUNNER.md ===\n# FEATURE: Test Runner\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | `bin/test.sh` runs the project suite against a temporary database and accepts an optional slice selector. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | `sh bin/test.sh [SLICE]` |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`bin/test.sh` is the single entry point for the project's automated tests. The terminal story runs the whole suite.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `sh bin/test.sh` runs `pytest -q` from the application root with no selector.\n- `sh bin/test.sh SLICE` passes `SLICE` to pytest as a `-k` INTEGER NOT NULL | Signed amount in integer cents |\n| memo | VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL DEFAULT '' | Memo, 0 to 60 characters |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n- None. Drydock owns SQLite schema construction and connectivity invariants deterministically.\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Money is never stored as `REAL` or `NUMERIC`. Every amount is `INTEGER` cents.\n- Application code never imports a database driver. It reaches the tables only through\n  `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-DATABASE-SELECTION.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Selection\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Resolves the database location from the environment and reports connection failures as persistence errors. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TEST-RUNNER.md |\n| Provides    | resolve_dsn(environ) -> str |\n| Consumes    | open_database(dsn) |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery command opens the database at the location the environment names. Resolution lives in\n`config.py`, and the persistence library then selects the engine from the DSN.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `resolve_dsn(environ)` returns the value of `DATABASE_URL` when it is set and non-empty.\n- Otherwise it returns the value of `DATABASE_PATH` when that is set and non-empty.\n- Otherwise it returns `data/app.db`.\n- The returned value is the configured string unchanged. No normalization is applied.\n- A `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN selects PostgreSQL. Any other value selects SQLite, with an\n  optional `sqlite://` prefix. The persistence library makes that choice.\n- Connection failures and expired connect attempts surface as `PersistenceError`, so the command\n  layer can exit `1`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC db-selection-url-precedence ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL wins over DATABASE_PATH when both are set, and the value is returned unchanged.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nurl = os.path.join(directory, \"url.db\")\npath = os.path.join(directory, \"path.db\")\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": url, \"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == url\n=== END AC db-selection-url-precedence ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-path-fallback ===\nIntent: DATABASE_PATH is used when DATABASE_URL is unset or empty.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(directory, \"path.db\")\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == path\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": \"\", \"DATABASE_PATH\": path}) == path\n=== END AC db-selection-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-postgres-dsn ===\nIntent: A PostgreSQL DSN in DATABASE_URL is returned unchanged for the persistence library to select the engine.\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\ndsn = \"postgresql://127.0.0.1:1/ledger\"\nassert resolve_dsn({\"DATABASE_URL\": dsn}) == dsn\n=== END AC db-selection-postgres-dsn ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-default ===\nIntent: With neither variable set, resolution returns a non-empty database location.\n\nfrom config import resolve_dsn\n\nresult = resolve_dsn({})\nassert isinstance(result, str)\nassert result != \"\"\n=== END AC db-selection-default ===\n\n=== AC db-selection-unreachable ===\nIntent: An unreachable PostgreSQL server raises PersistenceError instead of returning a database.\n\nRequires: python-package=psycopg; scope=runtime\n\nfrom persistence import PersistenceError, open_database\n\ndsn = \"postgresql://127.0.0.1:1/ledger\"\ntry:\n    db = open_database(dsn)\n    db.accounts.list()\nexcept PersistenceError as error:\n    failure = error\nelse:\n    failure = None\nprint(failure)\nassert isinstance(failure, PersistenceError)\n=== END AC db-selection-unreachable ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `config.py` is the only module that reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH`.\n- Diagnostics never print a DSN password to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-AMOUNT-CENTS.md ===\n# FEATURE: Amount Cents\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Converts between validated amount text and integer cents using integer arithmetic only. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-TEST-RUNNER.md |\n| Provides    | parse_amount(text) -> int; format_cents(cents) -> str |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAmounts are stored as integer cents and shown with two decimal places. This module is the only place\nthat converts between the two forms.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `parse_amount(text)` accepts text matching `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, with at most two decimal places, and\n  returns the signed integer cent value. `12.5` is `1250` cents.\n- `parse_amount` raises `ValueError` for any other text, including text with more than two decimal\n  places and text containing a comma.\n- `format_cents(cents)` returns text with exactly two decimal places, a leading `-` when negative,\n  and no thousands separator.\n- All arithmetic is integer arithmetic. No float or `Decimal` conversion is used at any point.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC amount-round-trip ===\nIntent: Formatting then parsing returns the original integer cents, and parse returns an int.\n\nfrom amounts import format_cents, parse_amount\n\nfor cents in (0, 1, -1, 1250, -1250, 500000, -2299):\n    text = format_cents(cents)\n    assert isinstance(text, str)\n    assert parse_amount(text) == cents\n    assert type(parse_amount(text)) is int\n=== END AC amount-round-trip ===\n\n=== AC amount-format-shape ===\nIntent: format_cents writes exactly two decimal places, a leading minus only when negative, and no thousands separator.\n\nfrom amounts import format_cents\n\nfor cents in (0, 5, -5, 1250, 500000, -1250000):\n    text = format_cents(cents)\n    whole, dot, fraction = text.lstrip(\"-\").partition(\".\")\n    assert dot == \".\"\n    assert len(fraction) == 2\n    assert fraction.isdigit()\n    assert whole.isdigit()\n    assert \",\" not in text\n    assert text.startswith(\"-\") == (cents < 0)\n=== END AC amount-format-shape ===\n\n=== AC amount-parse-decimal ===\nIntent: parse_amount reads a signed one-decimal amount as integer cents.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"-12.5\"\nassert parse_amount(text) == -1250\n=== END AC amount-parse-decimal ===\n\n=== AC amount-rejects-three-decimals ===\nIntent: parse_amount rejects text with more than two decimal places by raising ValueError.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"1.234\"\ntry:\n    parse_amount(text)\nexcept ValueError:\n    rejected = True\nelse:\n    rejected = False\nassert rejected is True\n=== END AC amount-rejects-three-decimals ===\n\n=== AC amount-rejects-separator ===\nIntent: parse_amount rejects text containing a thousands separator by raising ValueError.\n\nfrom amounts import parse_amount\n\ntext = \"1,000.00\"\ntry:\n    parse_amount(text)\nexcept ValueError:\n    rejected = True\nelse:\n    rejected = False\nassert rejected is True\n=== END AC amount-rejects-separator ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Never convert money through `float` at any layer.\n- Output amounts never carry a thousands separator.\n=== END ARTIFACT
